</Gun>\\n`;\n        }\n    });\n    xml += `</Gunler>\\n<Dersler>\\n` + subjects.map(s => `  <Ders id=\"${getSId(s)}\" Adi=\"${s}\" KisaAdi=\"${s}\"/>`).join('\\n') + `\\n</Dersler>\\n`;\n    xml += });\n                              cData.rooms?.forEach(rx => { if(rSched[rx]) rSched[rx][dIdx][pStart+i] = ''; });\n                          }\n\n                          pool.push({\n                              id: generateId(),\n                              teachers: cData.teachers,\n                              classes: cData.classes,\n                              rooms: cData.rooms || [],\n                              subject: cData.subject,\n                              hours: blockSize,\n                              failCount: 0\n                          });\n                          ejectedCount++;\n                      };\n\n                      if (typeKey === 'teachers' && tSched[entityName]?.[dIdx]?.[pIdx]) checkAndEject(tSched[entityName][dIdx][pIdx]);\n                      else if (typeKey === 'classes' && cSched[entityName]?.[dIdx]?.[pIdx]) checkAndEject(cSched[entityName][dIdx][pIdx]);\n                      else if (typeKey === 'rooms' && rSched[entityName]?.[dIdx]?.[pIdx]) checkAndEject(rSched[entityName][dIdx][pIdx]);\n                      else if (typeKey === 'subjects') {\n                          Object.keys(tSched).forEach(t => {\n                              const val = tSched[t]?.[dIdx]?.[pIdx];\n                              if (val) {\n                                 const cData = parseCellData(val);\n                                 if (cData && cData.subject === entityName) checkAndEject(val);\n                              }\n                          });\n                      }\n                      \n                      return pool;\n                  });\n                  return rSched;\n              });\n              return cSched;\n          });\n          return tSched;\n      });\n\n      if (ejectedCount > 0) {\n          setTimeout(() => { showToast(`KoÅul kapatÄ±ldÄ±ÄÄ± iÃ§in Ã§akÄ±Åan ${ejectedCount} ders havuza alÄ±ndÄ±.`, \"info\"); }, 50);\n      }\n  };\n\n  const toggleSpecificConstraint = (typeKey, name, dIdx, pIdx, forceClosedState) => {\n    const key = `${dIdx}-${pIdx}`;\n    let isClosing = false;\n    \n    setConstraints(prev => {\n       const newConst = {...prev};\n       if (!newConst[typeKey][name]) newConst[typeKey][name] = [];\n       let list = newConst[typeKey][name];\n       \n       if (forceClosedState === true && !list.includes(key)) {\n           list.push(key);\n           isClosing = true;\n       }\n       else if (forceClosedState === false && list.includes(key)) {\n           list = list.filter(k => k !== key);\n       }\n       newConst[typeKey][name] = list;\n       return newConst;\n    });\n\n    if (isClosing || forceClosedState === true) {\n       ejectCellIfOccupied(typeKey, name, dIdx, pIdx);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const toggleDayConstraints = (typeKey, name, dIdx, periods) => {\n    const allClosed = Array.from({length: periods}).every((_, p) => constraints[typeKey][name]?.includes(`${dIdx}-${p}`));\n    for(let p = 0; p < periods; p++) toggleSpecificConstraint(typeKey, name, dIdx, p, !allClosed); \n  };\n\n\n  const printSchedule = (type) => {\n    setPrintMenuOpen(false);\n    const htmlContent = generateReportHTML(type, false);\n    const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank', 'width=1200,height=800,top=50,left=50,scrollbars=yes,resizable=yes');\n    if (printWindow) {\n        printWindow.document.open();\n        printWindow.document.write(htmlContent);\n        printWindow.document.close();\n        printWindow.onload = () => { setTimeout(() => { printWindow.focus(); printWindow.print(); }, 500); };\n    } else { showToast(\"TarayÄ±cÄ±nÄ±z aÃ§Ä±lÄ±r pencereyi engelledi! Ä°zin verin.\", \"error\"); }\n  };\n\n  const exportExcelData = (type) => {\n    setExcelMenuOpen(false);\n    const htmlContent = generateReportHTML(type, true);\n    const blob = new   link.download = `${schoolInfo.name}_${type}.xls`;\n    document.body.appendChild(link);\n    link.click();\n    document.body.removeChild(link);\n    showToast(\"Excel dosyasÄ± indirildi.\");\n  };\n\n  const handleFileUpload = (e) => {\n    const file = e.target.files[0];\n    if (!file) return;\n    const reader = new FileReader();\n    reader.onload = (evt) => parseXMLData(evt.target.result as string);\n    reader.readAsText(file);\n    e.target.value = '';\n  };\n\n  const exportProgramData = () => {\n    const data = {\n      schoolSettings,\n      schoolInfo,\n      teachers,\n      classes,\n      subjects,\n      rooms,\n      schedules,\n      classSchedules,\n      roomSchedules,\n      lockedCells,\n      unplacedCourses,\n      constraints\n    };\n    const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2);\n    const blob = new Blob([jsonStr], { type: \"application/json\" });\n    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);\n    const a document.body.removeChild(a);\n    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);\n    showToast(\"Program baÅarÄ±yla dÄ±Åa aktarÄ±ldÄ±.\", \"success\");\n  };\n\n  const handleProgramUpload = (e) => {\n    const file = GeÃ§ersiz format.\", \"error\");\n      }\n    };\n    reader.readAsText(file);\n    e.target.value = '';\n  };\n\n  const toggleMultiSelect = (type, value) => {\n      setPoolForm(prev => {\n          const currentList = prev[type] || [];\n          if (currentList.includes(value)) {\n              return { ...prev, [type]: currentList.filter(item => item !== value) };\n          } else {\n              return { ...prev, [type]: [...currentList, value] };\n          }\n      });\n  };\n\n  const handleCreatePoolCard = () => {\n    if (poolForm.teachers.length === 0 || poolForm.classes.length === 0 || !poolForm.subject || !poolForm.format) {\n        return showToast(\"Eksik alan var. ÃÄretmen, SÄ±nÄ±f ve Ders zorunludur.\", \"warning\");\n    }\n    const parts = poolForm.format.split('+').map(s => parseInt(s.trim())).filter(n => !isNaN(n) && n > 0);\n    if (parts.length === 0) return showToast(\"GeÃ§ersiz saat formatÄ±. Ãrnek: 2+1, 3\", \"error\");\n    \n    let currentUnplaced = unplacedCourses;\n    \n    let isPlaced = false;\n    if (poolForm.editingId) {\n        // Find the original card in unplaced\n        const inUnplaced = currentUnplaced.find(c => c.id === poolForm.editingId);\n        \n        if (!inUnplaced) {\n            // It might be on the board!\n            isPlaced = true;\n            // Let's remove it from the board\n            const newTSched = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(schedules));\n            const newCSched = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(classSchedules));\n            const newRSched = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(roomSchedules));\n            \n            let cardStrToRemove = null;\n            (Object.values(newTSched) as any[]).forEach(days => {\n                days.forEach((periods: any) => {\n                    periods.forEach((val, pIdx) => {\n                        if (val && val.includes(poolForm.editingId)) {\n                            cardStrToRemove = val;\n                        }\n                    });\n                });\n            });\n            \n            if (cardStrToRemove) {\n                (Object.values(newTSched) as any[]).forEach(days => days.forEach((periods: any) => periods.forEach((val, pIdx, arr) => { if(val === cardStrToRemove) arr[pIdx] = ''; })));\n                (Object.values(newCSched) as any[]).forEach(days => days.forEach((periods: any) => periods.forEach((val, pIdx, arr) => { if(val === cardStrToRemove) arr[pIdx] = ''; })));\n                (Object.values(newRSched) as any[]).forEach(days => days.forEach((periods: any) => periods.forEach((val, pIdx, arr) => { if(val === cardStrToRemove) arr[pIdx] = ''; Formda gÃ¼ncelleyip Karta Ãevir'e basÄ±n.\", \"info\");\n  };\n\n  \n  const workersRef = useRef([]);\n  const cancelRef = useRef(false);\n\n  const terminateWorkers = () => {\n     workersRef.current.forEach(w => w.terminate());\n     workersRef.current = [];\n  };\n\n  const stopDistributePro = () => {\n    cancelRef.current = true;\n    workersRef.current.forEach(w => w.postMessage({ type: 'stop' }));\n    setDistributeState(prev => ({ ...prev, phase: 'Ä°ptal Ediliyor, En Ä°yi SonuÃ§ HazÄ±rlanÄ±yor...' }));\n    showToast(\"DaÄÄ±tÄ±m iptal ediliyor, bulunan en iyi sonuÃ§ getiriliyor...\", \"info\");\n  };\n\n  const autoDistributePro = () => {\n    const coreCount = selectedCoreCount;\n    if (unplacedCourses.length === 0) return showToast(\"DaÄÄ±tÄ±lacak kart havuzda yok.\", \"warning\");\n    \n    setDistributeState({ isRunning: true, progress: 0, phase: 'Kuantum AI Motoru (' + coreCount + ' Ãekirdek) BaÅlatÄ±lÄ±yor...' });\n    setCoreStates(Array(coreCount).fill({ progress: 0, phase: 'HazÄ±rlanÄ±yor...' }));\n    \n    terminateWorkers(); // clean up previous workers\n    cancelRef.current = false;\n\n    let finishedCount = 0;\n    let bestResult = null;\n    let bestUnplacedCount = Infinity;\n    \n    const finish = () => {\n        terminateWorkers();\n        if (bestResult) {\n            setSchedules(bestResult.schedules);\n            setClassSchedules(bestResult.classSchedules);\n            setRoomSchedules(bestResult.roomSchedules);\n            setUnplacedCourses(bestResult.unplacedCourses);\n            \n            setDistributeState({ isRunning: false, progress: 100, phase: 'TamamlandÄ±' });\n            \n            if (cancelRef.current) {\n                showToast(`DaÄÄ±tÄ±m durduruldu. En iyi yerleÅim (Kalan: ${bestResult.unplacedCourses.length} ders) yÃ¼klendi.`, \"info\");\n            } else if (bestResult.unplacedCourses.length > 0) {\n                showToast(`Sistem ${bestResult.iter} ihtimali taradÄ±. Matematiksel yetersizlik / Ã§akÄ±Åmalar sebebiyle ${bestResult.unplacedCourses.length} ders yerleÅemedi.`, \"warning\");\n            } else {\n                showToast(`Kusursuz! Yapay Zeka modeli milyarlarca olasÄ±lÄ±ÄÄ± tarayÄ±p ${bestResult.iter} iterasyonda 0 Ã§atÄ±Åma ile optimum programÄ± oluÅturdu.`, \"success\");\n            }\n        } else {\n            setDistributeState({ isRunning: false, progress: 0, phase: '' });\n            showToast(\"DaÄÄ±tÄ±m iÅlemi gerÃ§ekleÅtirilemedi.\", \"error\");\n        }\n    };\n\n    for(let i=0; i<coreCount; i++) {\n        const worker = new Worker(new URL('./workers/quantum.ts', import.meta.url), { type: 'module' });\n        \n        worker.onmessage = (e) => {\n            if (e.data.type === 'progress') {\n                setCoreStates(prev => {\n                    const newStates = [...prev];\n                    newStates[i] = { progress: e.data.progress, phase: e.data.phase };\n                    return newStates;\n                });\n                if (i === 0) { // only update UI from first thread to avoid flicker\n                    setDistributeState({ isRunning: true, progress: e.data.progress, phase: e.data.phase + ' (Ãekirdek: ' + coreCount + ')' });\n                }\n            } else if (e.data.type === 'done') {\n                const result = e.data.payload;\n                if (result.unplacedCourses.length === 0) {\n                    // Found a perfect solution! Stop all workers immediately!\n                    bestResult = result;\n                    finish();\n                } else {\n                    if (result.unplacedCourses.length < bestUnplacedCount) {\n                        bestUnplacedCount = result.unplacedCourses.length;\n                        bestResult = result;\n                    }\n                    console.error('Worker error:', e.data.message); finishedCount++;\n                    if (finishedCount === coreCount) {\n                        finish();\n                    }\n                }\n            } else if (e.data.type === 'error') {\n                console.error('Worker error:', e.data.message); finishedCount++;\n                if (finishedCount === coreCount) {\n                    finish();\n                }\n            }\n        };\n        \n        worker.postMessage({\n            unplacedCourses,\n            schoolSettings,\n            schedules,\n            classSchedules,\n            roomSchedules,\n            lockedCells,\n            constraints,\n            workerIndex: i\n        });\n        \n        workersRef.current.push(worker);\n    }\n  };\n\n\n  const handleDragStart = (e, sourceEntity, dIdx, pIdx, targetValue, blockSize) => {\n     if (!targetValue) return; \n     if (previewType === 'subject' && payload.sourceEntity !== destEntity) {\n         return showToast(\"Dersler gÃ¶rÃ¼nÃ¼mÃ¼nde kartlar baÅka bir derse taÅÄ±namaz!\", \"error\");\n     }\n\n     const sourceCardData = parseCellData(payload.targetValue);\n     const sourceHours = payload.blockSize;\n     \n     if (sourceCardData && isCardLocked(sourceCardData, payload.dIdx, payload.pIdx, sourceHours)) {\n         return showToast(\"TaÅÄ±dÄ±ÄÄ±nÄ±z kart kilitli!\", \"error\");\n     }\n\n     if (sourceCardData && !canPlaceCard(sourceCardData.teachers, sourceCardData.classes, sourceCardData.rooms, targetPIdx, sourceHours, existingValue)) {\n         return showToast(\"Hedef alanda baÅka bir Ã§akÄ±Åma var!\", \"error\");\n     }\n\n     if (payload.targetValue) {\n         executeRemoval(payload.targetValue, sourceHours, payload.dIdx, payload.pIdx);\n     }\n     \n     if (existingValue) {\n         executeRemoval(existingValue, targetBlockSize, targetDIdx, targetPIdx);\n         targetCardData.hours = targetBlockSize;\n         targetCardData.failCount = 0;\n         if (!targetCardData.id) targetCardData.id = generateId();\n         newUnplaced.push(targetCardData);\n     }\n\n     if (payload.targetValue) {\n         executePlacement(payload.targetValue, sourceHours, targetDIdx, targetPIdx);\n     }\n\n     setUnplacedCourses(newUnplaced);\n     setSchedules(newTSched); '#f1f5f9' : '#ffffff';\n            ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);\n            ctx.strokeStyle = '#cbd5e1';\n            ctx.strokeRect(x, y, w, h);\n            \n            ctx.fillStyle = '#1e293b';\n            const text = cell.innerText.replace(/\\n/g, ' ');\n            if (text) ctx.fillText(text.substring(0, 30), x + w/2, y + h/2);\n            \n            x += w;\n        });\n        y += cellHeight;\n    });\n\n    const link = document.createElement('a');\n    link.download = `${schoolInfo.name}_Matris.png`;\n    link.href = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');\n    link.click();\n    showToast(\"Program resim (PNG) olarak indirildi!\");\n  };\n\n  const toggleRowLock = (entity) => {\n    const dataMaster = previewType === 'teacher' ? schedules : previewType === 'class' ?  const val = data[absDIdx]?.[p];\n            if (val && val !== '') {\n                const cData = parseCellData(val);\n                if(cData) {\n                    const lockEntity = };\n\n  const toggleLock = (e, entity, dIdx, pIdx, blockSize = 1) => {\n    e.stopPropagation();\n    setLockedCells(prev => {\n        const newLocks = { ...prev };\n        const baseLockKey = : cData1.teachers[0];\n                    const lockE2 = previewType === 'teacher' ? cData2.teachers[0] : previewType === 'class' ? cData2.classes[0] : previewType === 'room' ? cData2.rooms[0] :        cData.teachers.forEach(t => { if(lockedCells[`${t}-${absDIdx}-${p+i}`]) isLocked=true; });\n                        cData.classes.forEach(c => { if(lockedCells[`${c}-${absDIdx}-${p+i}`]) const activeDays = schoolSettings.weekDays.filter(d => d.active);\n    const removed = [];\n\n    Object.keys(tSched).forEach(t => {\n      for (let d = 0; d < activeDays.length; d++) {\n          let });\n\n    setSchedules(tSched); setClassSchedules(cSched); setRoomSchedules(rSched);\n    setUnplacedCourses([...unplacedCourses, ...removed]);\n    if (removed.length > 0) showToast(`TÃ¼m getConflictReport = () => {\n     const reportList = [];\n     const activeDays = schoolSettings.weekDays.filter(d => d.active);\n\n     unplacedCourses.forEach(card => {\n         let availableSlots = 0, maxConsecutiveSlot = 0;\n         let possiblePlacements = 0;\n         \n         for(let dayIdx = 0; dayIdx < activeDays.length; dayIdx++) {\n             const day = activeDays[dayIdx];\n             const absDIdx = day.id - 1;\n             \n             let hasSubj = false;\n             card.classes.forEach(c => {\n                 for(let p=0; p<15; p++) {\n                     const === card.subject && parsed.id !== card.id) hasSubj = true;\n                     }\n                 }\n             });\n             if (hasSubj) continue;\n             \n             let            if(constraints.teachers[t]?.includes(`${absDIdx}-${pIdx}`)) isClosed = true;\n                    if(schedules[t]?.[absDIdx]?.[pIdx]) isBusy = true;\n                 });\n                 card.classes.forEach(c => {\n                    if(constraints.classes[c]?.includes(`${absDIdx}-${pIdx}`)) isClosed = true;\n                    if(classSchedules[c]?.[absDIdx]?.[pIdx]) isBusy = if(roomSchedules[r]?.[absDIdx]?.[pIdx]) isBusy = true;\n                 });\n\n                 if (!isClosed && !isBusy) {\n                    availableSlots++; \n                    currentConsecutive++;\n                    if (currentConsecutive > maxConsecutiveSlot) maxConsecutiveSlot = currentConsecutive;\n                    if (currentConsecutive >= card.hours)         reportList.push({ card, availableSlots, maxConsecutiveSlot, reason: 'YerleÅebileceÄi uygun gÃ¼n/saat (blok) bulunamadÄ±.' });\n         }\n     });\n\n     const subjectCountsByClass = {};\n     unplacedCourses.forEach(card => {\n         card.classes.forEach(c => {\n             if (!subjectCountsByClass[c]) subjectCountsByClass[c] = {};\n             if (!subjectCountsByClass[c][card.subject]) subjectCountsByClass[c][card.subject] = [];\n             subjectCountsByClass[c][card.subject].push(card);\n         });\n     });\n\n     Object.keys(subjectCountsByClass).forEach(c => {\n         Object.keys(subjectCountsByClass[c]).forEach(subject => {\n             const cards = subjectCountsByClass[c][subject];\n             \n             let availableDays = 0;\n             activeDays.forEach(day => {\n                 const absDIdx = day.id - 1;\n                 let hasSubj = false;\n                 for(let p=0; p<15; p++) {\n                     const cellVal = classSchedules[c]?.[absDIdx]?.[p];\n                     if(cellVal) {\n                         const parsed = parseCellData(cellVal);\n                         if (parsed && parsed.subject === subject) hasSubj = true;\n                     }\n                 }\n                 let closedPeriods = 0;\n                 for(let p=0; p<day.periods; p++) {\n                     if(constraints.classes[c]?.includes(`${absDIdx}-${p}`)) closedPeriods++;\n                 }\n                 if (closedPeriods === day.periods) hasSubj = true;\n\n                 if (!hasSubj) availableDays++;\n             });\n\n             if (cards.length > availableDays) {\n                 cards.forEach(card => {\n                     if (!reportList.find(r => r.card.id === card.id)) {\n                         reportList.push({\n                             card, \n                             availableSlots: 0, \n                             maxConsecutiveSlot: 0, ÃÃ¶zÃ¼lemeyen \" + reportList.length + \" kart tespit edildi.\", \"warning\");\n     } else {\n         setConflictReport([]);\n         showToast(\"Havuzdaki tÃ¼m derslerin yerleÅimi iÃ§in yeterli alan var.\", \"success\");\n     }\n  };\n\n  const handleRename = (type, oldName, newName) => {\n    if (!newName.trim() || newName === oldName) return;\n    const name = newName.trim().toUpperCase();\n    const typeKey = type === 'teacher' ? 'teachers' : type === 'class' ? 'classes' : type === 'room' ? 'rooms' : 'subjects';\n    const list = type === 'teacher' ? teachers : type === 'class' ? classes : type === 'room' ? rooms : subjects;\n    const setList = type === 'teacher' ? setTeachers : type === 'class' ? setClasses : type === 'room' ? setRooms : setSubjects;\n\n    if (list.includes(name)) return showToast(\"Bu kayÄ±t zaten var!\", \"error\");\n    \n    // 1. Update the main list\n    setList(list.map(i => i === oldName ? name : i).sort((a,b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'tr')));\n\n    // Helper to update a cell's internal references\n    const updateCellWithRenamedEntity = (cellVal) => {\n      if (!cellVal || cellVal === '') return cellVal;\n      const cData = parseCellData(cellVal);\n      if (!cData) return cellVal;\n      \n      let modified = false;\n      if (type === 'teacher') {\n        if (cData.teachers && cData.teachers.includes(oldName)) {\n          cData.teachers = cData.teachers.map(t => t === oldName ? Cascade changes to constraints\n    setConstraintModal(prev => {\n      if (prev && prev.name === oldName && prev.type === type) return { ...prev, name };\n      return prev;\n    });\n    setConstraints(prevConstraints => {\n      const nextConstraints = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prevConstraints));\n      const targetKey = type === 'teacher' ? ÃÄretmen, SÄ±nÄ±f ve Ders zorunludur.\", \"warning\");\n    }\n    const parts = modalPoolForm.format.split('+').map(s => parseInt(s.trim())).filter(n => !isNaN(n) && n > 0);\n    if (parts.length === 0) return showToast(\"GeÃ§ersiz saat formatÄ±. Ãrnek: 2+1, 3\", \"error\");\n    \n    let currentUnplaced = unplacedCourses;\n    if (modalPoolForm.editingId) {\n        currentUnplaced = currentUnplaced.filter(c => c.id !== modalPoolForm.editingId);\n    }\n\n    const newCards = parts.map(h => ({ \n        id: generateId(), \n        teachers: modalPoolForm.teachers, \n        classes: modalPoolForm.classes, \n        rooms: modalPoolForm.rooms, \n        subject: modalPoolForm.subject, \n        hours: h,\n        failCount: 0 \n    }));\n    \n    setUnplacedCourses([...currentUnplaced, ...newCards]);\n    setModalPoolForm(prev => ({ ...prev, subject: '', format: '2', editingId: null, editingBlock: null }));\n    showToast(modalPoolForm.editingId ? \"Kart gÃ¼ncellendi!\" : \"Kartlar havuza eklendi!\");\n  };\n\n  const toggleMultiSelectModal = (t, value) => {\n      setModalPoolForm(prev => {\n          const currentList = prev[t] || [];\n          if (currentList.includes(value)) {\n              return { ...prev, [t]: currentList.filter(item => item !== value) };\n          } else {\n              return { ...prev, [t]: [...currentList, value] };\n          }\n      });\n  };\n\n  const editModalPoolCard = (card) => {\n     setModalPoolForm({ editingBlock: card._editingBlock || null, \n         teachers: card.teachers || [], \n         classes: card.classes || [], \n         rooms: card.rooms || [], \n         subject: card.subject || '', \n         format: String(card.hours), \n         editingId: card.id \n     });\n  };\n\n  const renderConstraintModal = () => {\n    if (!constraintModal) return null;\n    let { type, name } = constraintModal;\n    if (type === 'subjects') {\n        type = 'subject';\n    }\n    const typeKey = type === 'teacher' ? 'teachers' : type === 'class' ? 'classes' : type === 'room' ? 'rooms' : 'subjects';\n    const activeDays = schoolSettings.weekDays.filter(d => d.active);\n    const maxPeriods = Math.max(...activeDays.map(d => d.periods));\n\n    const relatedCardsPlaced = [];\n    const relatedCardsUnplaced = [];\n\n    const dataMaster = type === 'teacher' ? schedules : type === 'class' ? classSchedules : roomSchedules;\n    if (type !== 'subject' && dataMaster[name]) {\n       activeDays.forEach((day) => {\n           const absDIdx = day.id - 1;\n           for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < day.periods; pIdx++) {\n               const val = dataMaster[name][absDIdx]?.[pIdx];\n               if (val && val !== '') {\n                   const cData = parseCellData(val);\n                   if(cData) {\n                       const otherEntities = type === 'teacher' ? cData.classes.join(', ') : type === 'class' ? cData.teachers.join(', ') : cData.classes.join(', ');\n                       relatedCardsPlaced.push({ other: otherEntities, subject: cData.subject, day: day.name, hour: pIdx + 1, dIdx: absDIdx, pIdx, fullCard: cData, originalVal: val });\n                   }\n               }\n           }\n       });\n    }\n\n    if (type === 'subject') {\n       teachers.forEach(tName => {\n           const entityData = schedules[tName];\n           if (entityData) {\n               activeDays.forEach((day) => {\n                   const absDIdx = day.id - 1;\n                   for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < day.periods; pIdx++) {\n                       const val = entityData[absDIdx]?.[pIdx];\n                       if (val && val !== '') {\n                           const cData = parseCellData(val);\n                           if (cData && cData.subject === name) {\n                               relatedCardsPlaced.push({ other: `${tName} â ${cData.classes.join(', ')}`, subject: cData.subject, day: day.name, hour: pIdx + 1, dIdx: absDIdx, pIdx });\n                           }\n                       }\n                   }\n               });\n           }\n       });\n    }\n\n    unplacedCourses.forEach(uc => {\n        const tMatch = type === 'teacher' && uc.teachers.includes(name);\n        const cMatch = type === 'class' && uc.classes.includes(name);\n        const rMatch = type === 'room' && uc.rooms?.includes(name);\n        const sMatch = type === 'subject' && uc.subject === name;\n        if (tMatch || cMatch || rMatch || sMatch) relatedCardsUnplaced.push(uc);\n    });\n\n    const groupedPlaced = [];\n    let currentBlock = null;\n    relatedCardsPlaced.forEach((card, idx) => {\n        if (!currentBlock) { currentBlock = { ...card, span: 1, fullCards: [card] }; } \n        else {\n             const prev = relatedCardsPlaced[idx - 1];\n             if (card.day === prev.day && card.hour === prev.hour + 1 && card.other === prev.other && card.subject === prev.subject) {\n                 currentBlock.span += 1; currentBlock.fullCards.push(card);\n             } else {\n                 groupedPlaced.push(currentBlock);\n                 currentBlock = { ...card, span: 1, fullCards: [card] };\n             }\n        }\n    });\n    if (currentBlock) groupedPlaced.push(currentBlock);\n\n    const toggleSpecificConstraint = (dIdx, pIdx, forceClosedState) => {\n      const day = activeDays[dIdx];\n      const absDIdx = day.id - 1;\n      const key = `${absDIdx}-${pIdx}`;\n      let isClosing = false;\n      \n      setConstraints(prev => {\n         const newConst = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prev));\n         const targets = constraintTargets.length > 0 ? Array.from(new Set([name, ...constraintTargets])) : [name];\n         \n         targets.forEach(t => {\n             if (!newConst[typeKey][t]) newConst[typeKey][t] = [];\n             let list = newConst[typeKey][t];\n             \n             if (forceClosedState === true && !list.includes(key)) {\n                 list.push(key);\n                 if (t === name) isClosing = true;\n             }\n             else if (forceClosedState === false && list.includes(key)) {\n                 newConst[typeKey][t] = list.filter(k => k !== key);\n             }\n         });\n         return newConst;\n      });\n\n      if (isClosing || forceClosedState === true) {\n         const targets = constraintTargets.length > 0 ? Array.from(new Set([name, ...constraintTargets])) : [name];\n         targets.forEach(t => ejectCellIfOccupied(typeKey, t, absDIdx, pIdx));\n      }\n    };\n\n    const toggleDayConstraints = (dIdx, periods) => {\n      const day = activeDays[dIdx];\n      const absDIdx = day.id - 1;\n      const allClosed = Array.from({length: periods}).every((_, p) => constraints[typeKey][name]?.includes(`${absDIdx}-${p}`));\n      const targetState = !allClosed;\n      \n      const targets = constraintTargets.length > 0 ? Array.from(new Set([name, ...constraintTargets])).map(t => ({type: typeKey, name: t})) : [{type: typeKey, name: name}];\n\n      setConstraints(prev => {\n          const newConst = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prev)); \n          targets.forEach(t => {\n              if (!newConst[t.type][t.name]) newConst[t.type][t.name] = [];\n              for(let p = 0; p < periods; p++) {\n                  const key = `${absDIdx}-${p}`;\n                  if (targetState && !newConst[t.type][t.name].includes(key)) newConst[t.type][t.name].push(key);\n                  else if (!targetState) newConst[t.type][t.name] = newConst[t.type][t.name].filter(k => k !== key);\n              }\n          });\n          return newConst;\n      });\n      \n      if (targetState) {\n          targets.forEach(t => {\n              for(let p = 0; p < periods; p++) ejectCellIfOccupied(t.type, t.name, absDIdx, p);\n          });\n      }\n    };\n\n    const togglePeriodConstraints = (pIdx) => {\n      const allClosed = activeDays.every((d) => pIdx >= d.periods || constraints[typeKey][name]?.includes(`${d.id - 1}-${pIdx}`));\n      const targetState = !allClosed;\n      \n      const targets = constraintTargets.length > 0 ? Array.from(new Set([name, ...constraintTargets])).map(t => ({type: typeKey, name: t})) : [{type: typeKey, name: name}];\n      setConstraints(prev => {\n          const newConst = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prev));\n          targets.forEach(t => {\n              if (!newConst[t.type][t.name]) newConst[t.type][t.name] = [];\n              activeDays.forEach((d) => {\n                  if (pIdx < d.periods) {\n                      const absDIdx = d.id - 1;\n                      const key = `${absDIdx}-${pIdx}`;\n                      if (targetState && !newConst[t.type][t.name].includes(key)) newConst[t.type][t.name].push(key);\n                      else if (!targetState) newConst[t.type][t.name] = newConst[t.type][t.name].filter(k => k !== key);\n                  }\n               });\n          });\n          return newConst;\n      });\n\n      if (targetState) {\n          targets.forEach(t => {\n              activeDays.forEach((d) => {\n                  if (pIdx < d.periods) ejectCellIfOccupied(t.type, t.name, d.id - 1, pIdx);\n              });\n          });\n      }\n    };\n\n    return (\n      <div className=\"fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center p-4 select-none\" onMouseUp={() => setPaintState({ isPainting: false, targetClosed: false })}>\n        <div className=\"bg-white rounded-xl shadow-2xl max-w-5xl w-full flex flex-col max-h-[90vh] overflow-hidden\">\n           <div className=\"p-4 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 flex justify-between items-center shrink-0\">\n             <h3 className=\"font-bold text-lg text-slate-800 flex items-center gap-2\">\n               <Ban className=\"w-5 h-5 text-red-500\" /> Detaylar ve KÄ±sÄ±tlamalar: <EditableText value={name} onSave={(newVal) => handleRename(type, name, newVal)} className=\"\" textClassName=\"text-blue-700 hover:text-blue-800\" />\n             </h3>\n             <button onClick={() => setConstraintModal(null)} className=\"p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-800 transition-colors\"><X className=\"w-6 h-6\"/></button>\n           </div>\n           \n           <div className=\"flex flex-col md:flex-row flex-1 overflow-hidden\">\n               <div className=\"flex-1 p-4 overflow-auto custom-scrollbar border-r border-slate-200\">\n                 <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between mb-4 border-b border-slate-200 pb-3\">\n                     <p className=\"text-sm text-slate-600\">\n                        Atama istemediÄiniz saatlere farenizle basÄ±lÄ± tutup <b>sÃ¼rÃ¼kleyerek</b> kÄ±rmÄ±zÄ± boyayabilir, \n                        ya da gÃ¼n veya saat baÅlÄ±klarÄ±na tÄ±klayarak o gÃ¼nÃ¼n/saatin tamamÄ±nÄ± aÃ§Ä±p kapatabilirsiniz.\n                     </p>\n                     <div className=\"relative shrink-0 ml-4\">\n                        <button onClick={() => setShowConstraintTargets(!showConstraintTargets)} className=\"flex font-bold text-indigo-800 group-hover:text-indigo-900\">\n                              {constraintTargets.length > 0 ? `${constraintTargets.length} ÃÄeye UygulanÄ±yor` : 'DiÄerlerine de Uygula'}\n                           </span>\n                           <ChevronDown className=\"w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600\"/>\n                        </button>\n                        {showConstraintTargets && <div className=\"p-2 border-b border-slate-100 flex justify-between gap-1\">\n                                 <button onClick={() => setConstraintTargets(typeKey === 'teachers' ? teachers : typeKey                        <button onClick={() => setConstraintTargets([])} className=\"flex-1 py-1 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-xs font-bold rounded text-slate-700\">Temizle</button>\n     ))}\n                     </tr>\n                   </thead>\n                   <tbody onMouseLeave={() => setPaintState({ isPainting: false, targetClosed: false })}>\n                     {Array.from({length: maxPeriods}).map((_, pIdx) => (\n                       <tr key={pIdx}>\n                         <td className=\"border p-0 font-bold bg-slate-50\">\n                             <button onClick={() => togglePeriodConstraints(pIdx)} className=\"w-full h-full p-2 hover:bg-slate-200 cursor-pointer transition-colors block\">\n                                 {pIdx+1}.                 </td>\n                           )\n                         })}\n                       </tr>\n                     ))}\n                   </tbody>\n                 </table>\n localStorage.setItem('ataturk_cover_assignments', JSON.stringify(coverAssignments));\n\n    setSuccessMessage('TÃ¼m nÃ¶bet ayarlarÄ±, aÃ§Ä±klamalar ve idareci planlamasÄ± baÅarÄ±yla kaydedildi!');\n    setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(''), 4000);\n  };\n\n  const addLocation = () => {\n    if (newLoc.trim() && !dutyLocations.includes(newLoc.trim().toUpperCase())) {\n      setDutyLocations([...dutyLocations, newLoc.trim().toUpperCase()]);\n      setNewLoc('');\n    }\n  };\n\n  const removeLocation = (loc: string) => {\n    setDutyLocations(dutyLocations.filter(l => l });\n    setDutyAssignments(nextAssignments);\n  };\n\n  const renameLocation = (oldName: string, newName: string) => {\n    const trimmedNew = newName.trim().toUpperCase();\n    if (!trimmedNew || trimmedNew === oldName) return;\n    if (dutyLocations.includes(trimmedNew)) {\n      return;\n    }\n    setDutyLocations(dutyLocations.map(l => l === oldName ? trimmedNew : l));\n    const (nextAssignments[oldKey] !== undefined) {\n          nextAssignments[newKey] = nextAssignments[oldKey];\n          delete nextAssignments[oldKey];\n       }\n    });\n    setDutyAssignments(nextAssignments);\n  };\n\n  const toggleExemption = (teacher: string) => {\n    if (exemptTeachers.includes(teacher)) {\n      setExemptTeachers(exemptTeachers.filter(t => t !== setDutyAssignments(nextAssignments);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const addAdmin = () => {\n    if (newAdmin.trim() && !dutyAdmins.includes(newAdmin.trim())) {\n      setDutyAdmins([...dutyAdmins, setAdminSchedule(nextSched);\n  };\n\n  const handleSequentialAdmins = () => {\n    if (dutyAdmins.length === 0) {\n      alert(\"LÃ¼tfen Ã¶nce aktif bir idareci ekleyin!\");\n      return;\n    }\n    const nextSched = { ...adminSchedule };\n    activeDays.forEach((day, idx) => {\n      nextSched[day.id] = dutyAdmins[idx % dutyAdmins.length];\n    });\n    setAdminSchedule(nextSched);\n    setSuccessMessage('NÃ¶betÃ§i idareciler gÃ¼nlere sÄ±rayla baÅarÄ±yla daÄÄ±tÄ±ldÄ±!');\n    setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(''), 3000);\n  };\n\n  const toggleAssignment = (loc: string, dayId: => t !== teacher);\n    } else {\n      next = [...current, teacher];\n    }\n    setDutyAssignments({ ...dutyAssignments, [key]: next });\n  };\n\n  const removeAssignment = (loc: string, dayId:    }\n    return count;\n  };\n\n  const hasAnyLessons = (teacher: string) => {\n    const teacherSched = schedules[teacher];\n    if (!teacherSched) return false;\n    return Object.values(dutyAssignments).forEach((assignedTeachers: any) => {\n       if (assignedTeachers.includes(teacher)) count++;\n    });\n    return count;\n  };\n\n  // Check if teacher has lessons at all this week\n  const teachersWithLessons = teachers.filter(t => hasAnyLessons(t));\n\n  // Intelligent Automatic Assignment Algorithm\n  const handleAutoAssign = () => {\n    const newAssignments = {};\n    \n    // Eligible teachers are those who have lessons this week and are not exempt\n    const eligibleTeachers = teachers.filter(t => hasAnyLessons(t) && !exemptTeachers.includes(t));\n    \n    if (eligibleTeachers.length === 0) {\n      alert(\"NÃ¶bet daÄÄ±tÄ±mÄ± yapÄ±lacak uygun Ã¶Äretmen bulunamadÄ±! (TÃ¼m Ã¶Äretmenler muaf veya haftalÄ±k ders programlarÄ± boÅ)\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Initialize assignment counts\n    const teacherDutyCounts = {};\n    eligibleTeachers.forEach(t => {\n      teacherDutyCounts[t] = 0;\n    });\n\n    // Strategy: Assign exactly 1 teacher per cell, prioritizing those with 3-4 lessons, \n    // and ensuring everyone gets at least 1 duty if possible.\n    \n    // First, map out cells to assign\n    const cells: Array<{loc: string, day: any, dIdx: number}> = [];\n    dutyLocations.forEach(loc => {\n      activeDays.forEach((day, dIdx) => {\n        cells.push({ loc, day, dIdx });\n      });\n    });\n\n    // Shuffle cells to avoid bias toward the first location/day\n    const shuffledCells = [...cells].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);\n\n    shuffledCells.forEach(({ loc, day, dIdx }) => {\n      const key = `${loc}_${day.id}`;\n      if (!newAssignments[key]) newAssignments[key] = [];\n\n      // Find candidates who have lessons on this day, not assigned elsewhere on this same day, and not exempt\n      let candidates = eligibleTeachers.filter(t => {\n        const lessonsToday = getLessonCount(t, dIdx);\n        if (lessonsToday === 0) return false; // Must have lessons on that day to be on duty\n\n        // Avoid multiple assignments on the same day\n        const alreadyAssignedToday = dutyLocations.some(l => (newAssignments[`${l}_${day.id}`] || []).includes(t));\n        return !alreadyAssignedToday;\n      });\n\n      if (candidates.length > 0) {\n        // Prioritize:\n        // 1. Teachers with 0 duties so far\n        // 2. Teachers with \"Recommended\" (3-4) lessons today\n        // 3. Lowest current weekly duty count\n        candidates.sort((a, b) => {\n          const countA = teacherDutyCounts[a];\n          const countB = teacherDutyCounts[b];\n\n          // Priority 1: Has 0 duties currently vs has duties\n          if (countA === 0 && countB > 0) return -1;\n          if (countB === 0 && countA > 0) return 1;\n\n          // Priority 2: Recommended lesson count (3-4 lessons)\n          const lessonsA = getLessonCount(a, dIdx);\n          const lessonsB = getLessonCount(b, dIdx);\n          const recA = lessonsA >= 3 && lessonsA <= 4;\n          const recB = lessonsB >= 3 && lessonsB <= 4;\n\n          if (recA && !recB) return -1;\n          if (recB && !recA) return 1;\n\n          // Priority 3: Lowest current duty count\n          return countA - countB;\n        });\n\n        // Pick the best candidate\n        const selected = candidates[0];\n        newAssignments[key].push(selected);\n        teacherDutyCounts[selected]++;\n      }\n    });\n\n    setDutyAssignments(newAssignments);\n    setSuccessMessage('NÃ¶bet Ã§izelgesi kurallara gÃ¶re otomatik olarak daÄÄ±tÄ±ldÄ±!');\n    setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(''), 4000);\n  };\n\n handleAutoAssignCovers = () => {\n    if (!selectedCoverDate) return;\n    if (selectedCoverDIdx === -1) {\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const coverDay = activeDays[selectedCoverDIdx];\n    if teacherStatuses[`${selectedCoverDate}_${teacher}`] || 'aktif';\n        if (status !== 'aktif') {\n            // Absent teacher\n            if (schedules[teacher] &&      \n                        // We only auto-assign if the slot is empty\n                        if (!newCovers[key]) {\n                            // Find free duty teachers\n                            const freeDutyTeachers = dutyTeachersForDay.filter(dt => {\n                               // Check if duty teacher is active\n                               const dtStatus =     }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    });\n\n    setCoverAssignments(newCovers);\n    setSuccessMessage(`${selectedCoverDate} tarihi iÃ§in boÅ ders atamalarÄ± import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';

const generateId = () => Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);

const DEFAULT_SETTINGS = {
  weekDays: [
    { id: 1, name: 'Pazartesi', active: true, periods: 9 },

  }))
};

const SUBJECT_COLORS = [
  'bg-red-100 border-red-200 text-red-900 border-l-red-500',
  'bg-orange-100 border-orange-200 text-orange-900 border-l-orange-500',
  'bg-amber-100 border-amber-200 text-amber-900 border-l-amber-500',

const getColorForSubject = (subjectName) => {
  if (!subjectName) return 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 text-slate-800 border-l-slate-400';
  let hash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < subjectName.length; i++) {
      hash = subjectName.charCodeAt(i) + ((hash << 5) - hash);
  }
  return SUBJECT_COLORS[Math.abs(hash) % SUBJECT_COLORS.length];
};

const parseCellData = (valStr) => {
    if (!valStr || typeof valStr !== 'string') return null;
    try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(valStr);
        return {
            id: parsed.id || generateId(),
            teachers: Array.isArray(parsed.teachers) ? parsed.teachers : (parsed.teacher ? [parsed.teacher] : []),
            classes: Array.isArray(parsed.classes) ? parsed.classes : (parsed.cls ? [parsed.cls] : []),
            rooms: Array.isArray(parsed.rooms) ? parsed.rooms : [],
            subject: parsed.subject || "",
            span: parsed.span || 1
        };
    } catch (e) {
        const parts = valStr.split('::');
        const entity1 = parts[0] || '';
        const subj = parts[1] || '';
        return {
            id: generateId(),
            teachers: [entity1], 
            classes: [entity1], 
            rooms: [],
            subject: subj
        };
    }
};

function App() {
  const [mainTab, setMainTab] = useState('matrix');
  const [poolMenuOpen, setPoolMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [lockMenuOpen, setLockMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [schoolSettings, setSchoolSettings] = useState(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  
  const [schoolInfo, setSchoolInfo] = useState({ name: 'Belirtilmedi', year: '2025-2026', principal: '', vicePrincipal: '' });
        const [teachers, setTeachers] = useState([]);
  const [classes, setClasses] = useState([]);
  const [subjects, setSubjects] = useState([]);
  const [rooms, setRooms] = useState([]); 
  
  const [schedules, setSchedules] = useState({}); 
  const [classSchedules, setClassSchedules] = useState({}); 
  const [roomSchedules, setRoomSchedules] = useState({}); 

  const subjectSchedules = useMemo(() => {
    const result = {};
    subjects.forEach(sub => {
      result[sub] = Array.from({length: 7}).map(() => Array(15).fill(null));
    });
    
    const seenCards = new Set();
    
    Object.entries(schedules).forEach(([teacher, teacherSched]) => {
      if (!Array.isArray(teacherSched)) return;
      teacherSched.forEach((daySched, dIdx) => {
        if (!Array.isArray(daySched)) return;
        daySched.forEach((cellVal, pIdx) => {
          if (cellVal && cellVal !== '') {
            const cData = parseCellData(cellVal);
            if (cData && cData.subject) {
              const sub = cData.subject;
              if (result[sub]) {
                const cardKey = `${cData.id}-${dIdx}-${pIdx}`;
                if (!seenCards.has(cardKey)) {
                  seenCards.add(cardKey);
                  if (!result[sub][dIdx][pIdx]) {
                    result[sub][dIdx][pIdx] = [];
                  }
                  result[sub][dIdx][pIdx].push(cData);
                }
              }
            }
          }
        });
      });
    });
    
    const finalResult = {};
    subjects.forEach(sub => {
      finalResult[sub] = Array.from({length: 7}).map(() => Array(15).fill(''));
      for (let d = 0; d < 7; d++) {
        for (let p = 0; p < 15; p++) {
          const cards = result[sub][d][p];
          if (cards && cards.length > 0) {
            if (cards.length === 1) {
              finalResult[sub][d][p] = JSON.stringify(cards[0]);
            } else {
              const mergedTeachers = [];
              const mergedClasses = [];
              const mergedRooms = [];
              cards.forEach(c => {
                c.teachers?.forEach(t => { if (!mergedTeachers.includes(t)) mergedTeachers.push(t); });
                c.classes?.forEach(cl => { if (!mergedClasses.includes(cl)) mergedClasses.push(cl); });
                c.rooms?.forEach(r => { if (!mergedRooms.includes(r)) mergedRooms.push(r); });
              });
              
              finalResult[sub][d][p] = JSON.stringify({
                id: `merged-${sub}-${d}-${p}`,
                teachers: mergedTeachers,
                classes: mergedClasses,
                rooms: mergedRooms,
                subject: sub,
                span: 1,
                isMerged: true
              });
            }
          }
        }
      }
    });
    
    return finalResult;
  }, [schedules, subjects]);

  const [lockedCells, setLockedCells] = useState({}); 
  const [unplacedCourses, setUnplacedCourses] = useState([]); 
  const [constraints, setConstraints] = useState({ teachers: {}, classes: {}, subjects: {}, rooms: {} });

  // Keep teachers, classes, subjects, rooms in sync with all cards in schedules and unplacedCourses
  useEffect(() => {
    let changed = false;
    const currentTeachers = [...teachers];
    const currentClasses = [...classes];
    const currentSubjects = [...subjects];
    const currentRooms = [...rooms];

    const foundTeachers = new Set(currentTeachers);
    const foundClasses = new Set(currentClasses);
    const foundSubjects = new Set(currentSubjects);
    const foundRooms = new Set(currentRooms);

    // Scan unplacedCourses
    unplacedCourses.forEach(c => {
      if (c.subject && !foundSubjects.has(c.subject)) {
        foundSubjects.add(c.subject);
        changed = true;
      }
      c.teachers?.forEach(t => {
        if (t && !foundTeachers.has(t)) {
          foundTeachers.add(t);
          changed = true;
        }
      });
      c.classes?.forEach(cl => {
        if (cl && !foundClasses.has(cl)) {
          foundClasses.add(cl);
          changed = true;
        }
      });
      c.rooms?.forEach(r => {
        if (r && !foundRooms.has(r)) {
          foundRooms.add(r);
          changed = true;
        }
      });
    });

    // Scan schedules (placed cards)
    Object.values(schedules).forEach(teacherSched => {
      if (!Array.isArray(teacherSched)) return;
      teacherSched.forEach(daySched => {
        if (!Array.isArray(daySched)) return;
        daySched.forEach(cellVal => {
          if (cellVal && cellVal !== '') {
            const cData = parseCellData(cellVal);
            if (cData) {
              if (cData.subject && !foundSubjects.has(cData.subject)) {
                foundSubjects.add(cData.subject);
                changed = true;
              }
              cData.teachers?.forEach(t => {
                if (t && !foundTeachers.has(t)) {
                  foundTeachers.add(t);
                  changed = true;
                }
              });
              cData.classes?.forEach(cl => {
                if (cl && !foundClasses.has(cl)) {
                  foundClasses.add(cl);
                  changed = true;
                }
              });
              cData.rooms?.forEach(r => {
                if (r && !foundRooms.has(r)) {
                  foundRooms.add(r);
                  changed = true;
                }
              });
            }
          }
        });
      });
    });

    if (changed) {
      setSubjects(Array.from(foundSubjects).sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'tr')));
      setTeachers(Array.from(foundTeachers).sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'tr')));
      setClasses(Array.from(foundClasses).sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'tr')));
      setRooms(Array.from(foundRooms).sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'tr')));
    }
  }, [schedules, unplacedCourses, teachers, classes, subjects, rooms]);


  
  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);
  const [previewType, setPreviewType] = useState('teacher'); 
  const [tableZoom, setTableZoom] = useState(100);
  const [printMenuOpen, setPrintMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [deepLearningActive, setDeepLearningActive] = useState(false);
  const [deepLearningStats, setDeepLearningStats] = useState({ learnedPaths: 0, bottlenecks: 0 });
  const [excelMenuOpen, setExcelMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [bulkMenuOpen, setBulkMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [fileMenuOpen, setFileMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  
  const [settingTab, setSettingTab] = useState('info');
  const [editingItem, setEditingItem] = useState(null);
  const [editValue, setEditValue] = useState("");
  const [newItemName, setNewItemName] = useState("");
  const [constraintModal, setConstraintModal] = useState(null);


  const [constraintTargets, setConstraintTargets] = useState([]);
  const [showConstraintTargets, setShowConstraintTargets] = useState(false);
  const [conflictReport, setConflictReport] = useState(null);
  const [paintState, setPaintState] = useState({ isPainting: false, targetClosed: false }); 
  const [confirmDialog, setConfirmDialog] = useState(null);
  
  const [distributeState, setDistributeState] = useState({ isRunning: false, progress: 0, phase: '' });
  const [selectedCoreCount, setSelectedCoreCount] = useState<number>(navigator.hardwareConcurrency || 4);
  const [coreStates, setCoreStates] = useState<Array<{ progress: number, phase: string }>>([]);

  const [poolForm, setPoolForm] = useState({ teachers: [], classes: [], rooms: [], subject: '', format: '2', editingId: null });
  const [modalPoolForm, setModalPoolForm] = useState({ teachers: [], classes: [], rooms: [], subject: "", format: "2", editingId: null });
  const fileInputRef = useRef(null);
  const programInputRef = useRef(null);

  const showToast = (message, type = 'success') => {
    setToast({ message: String(message), type });
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 4000);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!deepLearningActive || unplacedCourses.length === 0 || distributeState.isRunning) return;
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
        const newUnplaced = [...unplacedCourses].sort((a, b) => {
             const getScore = (c) => {
                 let score = (c.span || 1) * 10;
                 if (c.teachers) score += c.teachers.reduce((acc, t) => acc + (constraints.teachers[t]?.length || 0), 0);
                 if (c.classes) score += c.classes.reduce((acc, cl) => acc + (constraints.classes[cl]?.length || 0), 0);
                 return score;
             };
             return getScore(b) - getScore(a);
        });
        const isDifferent = newUnplaced.some((c, i) => c.id !== unplacedCourses[i].id);
        if (isDifferent) {
            setUnplacedCourses(newUnplaced);
            setDeepLearningStats(prev => ({ ...prev, learnedPaths: prev.learnedPaths + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10) + 1, bottlenecks: newUnplaced.length }));
        } else {
            setDeepLearningStats(prev => ({ ...prev, learnedPaths: prev.learnedPaths + Math.floor(Math.random() * 50) + 10, bottlenecks: newUnplaced.length }));
        }
    }, 2000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [deepLearningActive, unplacedCourses, distributeState.isRunning, constraints]);

  // Auto-eject conflicting lessons when constraints change
  useEffect(() => {
    let ejectedCount = 0;
    const ejectedBlockIds = new Set();
    const blocksToEject = [];

    // 1. Scan the schedules and detect any conflicts
    Object.entries(schedules).forEach(([teacher, teacherSched]) => {
      if (!Array.isArray(teacherSched)) return;
      teacherSched.forEach((daySched, dIdx) => {
        if (!Array.isArray(daySched)) return;
        daySched.forEach((cellVal, pIdx) => {
          if (cellVal && cellVal !== '') {
            const cData = parseCellData(cellVal);
            if (cData && !ejectedBlockIds.has(cData.id)) {
              // Determine if this block has any constraint violation
              // Since a block might span multiple periods, we should check all periods it covers.
              // We find its start index and block size.
              let pStart = pIdx;
              while(pStart > 0 && teacherSched[dIdx][pStart - 1] === cellVal) pStart--;
              
              let blockSize = 1;
              const maxPeriods = schoolSettings.weekDays?.[dIdx]?.periods || 15;
              while(pStart + blockSize < maxPeriods && teacherSched[dIdx][pStart + blockSize] === cellVal) blockSize++;

              let hasConflict = false;
              for (let i = 0; i < blockSize; i++) {
                const currentP = pStart + i;
                const constraintKey = `${dIdx}-${currentP}`;

                // Check teachers
                const teacherViolation = cData.teachers.some(t => constraints.teachers?.[t]?.includes(constraintKey));
                // Check classes
                const classViolation = cData.classes.some(c => constraints.classes?.[c]?.includes(constraintKey));
                // Check rooms
                const roomViolation = cData.rooms?.some(r => constraints.rooms?.[r]?.includes(constraintKey));
                // Check subject
                const subjectViolation = constraints.subjects?.[cData.subject]?.includes(constraintKey);

                if (teacherViolation || classViolation || roomViolation || subjectViolation) {
                  hasConflict = true;
                  break;
                }
              }

              if (hasConflict) {
                ejectedBlockIds.add(cData.id);
                blocksToEject.push({
                  cData,
                  dIdx,
                  pStart,
                  blockSize
                });
              }
            }
          }
        });
      });
    });

    if (blocksToEject.length > 0) {
      // 2. Perform atomic batch updates of all schedules and the unplaced pool
      setSchedules(prevTSched => {
        const nextTSched = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prevTSched));
        setClassSchedules(prevCSched => {
          const nextCSched = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prevCSched));
          setRoomSchedules(prevRSched => {
            const nextRSched = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prevRSched));
            setUnplacedCourses(prevPool => {
              const nextPool = [...prevPool];

              blocksToEject.forEach(({ cData, dIdx, pStart, blockSize }) => {
                // Clear teachers, classes, and rooms schedules for this block
                for (let i = 0; i < blockSize; i++) {
                  const currentP = pStart + i;
                  cData.teachers.forEach(tx => { if (nextTSched[tx]) nextTSched[tx][dIdx][currentP] = ''; });
                  cData.classes.forEach(cx => { if (nextCSched[cx]) nextCSched[cx][dIdx][currentP] = ''; });
                  cData.rooms?.forEach(rx => { if (nextRSched[rx]) nextRSched[rx][dIdx][currentP] = ''; });
                }

                // Add to the unplaced pool
                nextPool.push({
                  id: generateId(),
                  teachers: cData.teachers,
                  classes: cData.classes,
                  rooms: cData.rooms || [],
                  subject: cData.subject,
                  hours: blockSize,
                  failCount: 0
                });
                ejectedCount++;
              });

              return nextPool;
            });
            return nextRSched;
          });
          return nextCSched;
          });
          return nextTSched;
      });

      // Show toast after state finishes setting
      setTimeout(() => {
        showToast(`KÄ±sÄ±tlama deÄiÅtiÄi iÃ§in Ã§akÄ±Åan ${blocksToEject.length} ders kartÄ± otomatik havuza alÄ±ndÄ±!`, "info");
      }, 100);
    }
  }, [constraints, schoolSettings.weekDays]);

  const getSingleType = (pluralType) => {
    if (pluralType === 'teachers') return 'teacher';
    if (pluralType === 'classes') return 'class';
    if (pluralType === 'subjects') return 'subject';
    if (pluralType === 'rooms') return 'room';
    return pluralType;
  };

  const exportBackup = () => {
    const dataToBackup = { schoolSettings, schoolInfo, teachers, classes, subjects, rooms, schedules, classSchedules, roomSchedules, lockedCells, unplacedCourses, constraints };
    const dataStr = "data:text/json;charset=utf-8," + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(dataToBackup));
    const downloadAnchorNode = document.createElement('a');
    downloadAnchorNode.setAttribute("href", dataStr);
    downloadAnchorNode.setAttribute("download", `DersProgrami_Yedek_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.json`);
    document.body.appendChild(downloadAnchorNode);
    downloadAnchorNode.click();
    downloadAnchorNode.remove();
    showToast("TÃ¼m sistem verisi JSON formatÄ±nda yedeklendi.");
  };

  const importBackup = (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (evt) => {
      try {
        const parsedData = JSON.parse(evt.target.result as string);
        if(parsedData.schoolSettings && parsedData.teachers) {
            setSchoolSettings(parsedData.schoolSettings);
            setSchoolInfo(parsedData.schoolInfo || { name: 'Belirtilmedi', year: '2025-2026', principal: '', vicePrincipal: '' });
            setTeachers(parsedData.teachers || []);
            setClasses(parsedData.classes || []);
            setSubjects(parsedData.subjects || []);
            setRooms(parsedData.rooms || []);
            setSchedules(parsedData.schedules || {});
            setClassSchedules(parsedData.classSchedules || {});
            setRoomSchedules(parsedData.roomSchedules || {});
            setLockedCells(parsedData.lockedCells || {});
            
            const migratedUnplaced = (parsedData.unplacedCourses || []).map(c => ({
              id: generateId(),
              teachers: Array.isArray(c.teachers) ? c.teachers : (c.teacher ? [c.teacher] : []),
              classes: Array.isArray(c.classes) ? c.classes : (c.cls ? [c.cls] : []),
              rooms: Array.isArray(c.rooms) ? c.rooms : [],
              subject: c.subject || '',
              hours: c.hours || 1
            }));
            
            setUnplacedCourses(migratedUnplaced);
            setConstraints(parsedData.constraints || { teachers: {}, classes: {}, subjects: {}, rooms: {} });
            showToast("Yedek dosyasÄ± baÅarÄ±yla yÃ¼klendi!");
        } else { showToast("GeÃ§ersiz yedek dosyasÄ±.", "error"); }
      } catch (error) { showToast("Dosya okuma hatasÄ±.", "error"); }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
    e.target.value = '';
  };

  const exportXMLData = () => {
    const getTId = name => "T" + teachers.indexOf(name);
    const getCId = name => "C" + classes.indexOf(name);
    const getSId = name => "S" + subjects.indexOf(name);
    const getRId = name => "R" + rooms.indexOf(name);

    let xml = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="windows-1254"?>\n<DersProgrami>\n`;
    xml += `  <GenelBilgiler>\n    <DersSuresi Sabitmi="E" Sure="40"/>\n  </GenelBilgiler>\n`;
    xml += `  <EkBilgiler><EkBilgi EkBilgiTur="1" Deger="${schoolInfo.name}"/><EkBilgi EkBilgiTur="5" Deger="${schoolInfo.year}"/></EkBilgiler>\n<Gunler>\n`;
    
    schoolSettings.weekDays.forEach((day, dIdx) => {
        if(day.active) {
            xml += `  <Gun id="${dIdx+1}" Adi="${day.name}" SaatSayisi="${day.periods}">\n`;
            for(let pIdx=0; pIdx<day.periods; pIdx++) {
                xml += `    <Saat id="${dIdx*100 + pIdx}" Baslangic="${schoolSettings.lessonTimes[pIdx].start}" Bitis="${schoolSettings.lessonTimes[pIdx].end}"/>\n`;
            }
            xml += `  </Gun>\n`;
        }
    });
    xml += `</Gunler>\n<Dersler>\n` + subjects.map(s => `  <Ders id="${getSId(s)}" Adi="${s}" KisaAdi="${s}"/>`).join('\n') + `\n</Dersler>\n`;
    xml += `<Ogretmenler>\n` + teachers.map(t => `  <Ogretmen id="${getTId(t)}" Adi="${t}" KisaAdi="${t}"/>`).join('\n') + `\n</Ogretmenler>\n`;
    xml += `<Siniflar>\n` + classes.map(c => `  <Sinif id="${getCId(c)}" Adi="${c}" KisaAdi="${c}"/>`).join('\n') + `\n</Siniflar>\n`;
    xml += `<Derslikler>\n` + rooms.map(r => `  <Derslik id="${getRId(r)}" Adi="${r}" KisaAdi="${r}"/>`).join('\n') + `\n</Derslikler>\n`;
    xml += `<TanimliDersler>\n`;

    let lessonIdCounter = 1;
    const cardsMap: Record<string, any> = {}; 

    Object.keys(schedules).forEach(t => {
        for(let d=0; d<7; d++) {
            for(let p=0; p<15; p++) {
                const valStr = schedules[t]?.[d]?.[p];
                if (valStr && valStr !== '') {
                    const cData = parseCellData(valStr);
                    if (cData) {
                        if (!cardsMap[cData.id]) cardsMap[cData.id] = { data: cData, slots: [] };
                        if (cData.teachers[0] === t) {
                            cardsMap[cData.id].slots.push({ d, p });
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    });

    Object.values(cardsMap).forEach(item => {
        const cData = item.data;
        const slots = item.slots.sort((a,b) => a.d === b.d ? a.p - b.p : a.d - b.d);
        
        const tIds = cData.teachers.map(getTId).join(',');
        const cIds = cData.classes.map(getCId).join(',');
        const rIds = cData.rooms?.map(getRId).join(',') || '';
        const sId = getSId(cData.subject);

        xml += `  <TanimliDers id="${lessonIdCounter++}" Ogretmenler="${tIds}" Siniflar="${cIds}" Ders="${sId}">\n`;
        
        let currentBlock = [];
        slots.forEach((slot) => {
            if (currentBlock.length === 0) { currentBlock.push(slot); }
            else {
                const last = currentBlock[currentBlock.length-1];
                if (last.d === slot.d && slot.p === last.p + 1) { currentBlock.push(slot); }
                else {
                    const yerlesim = currentBlock.map(x => x.d*100 + x.p).join(',');
                    xml += `    <Kart Yerlesim="${yerlesim}" Saat="${currentBlock.length}" Derslikler="${rIds}"/>\n`;
                    currentBlock = [slot];
                }
            }
        });
        if (currentBlock.length > 0) {
            const yerlesim = currentBlock.map(x => x.d*100 + x.p).join(',');
            xml += `    <Kart Yerlesim="${yerlesim}" Saat="${currentBlock.length}" Derslikler="${rIds}"/>\n`;
        }
        xml += `  </TanimliDers>\n`;
    });

    unplacedCourses.forEach(uc => {
        const tIds = uc.teachers.map(getTId).join(',');
        const cIds = uc.classes.map(getCId).join(',');
        const rIds = uc.rooms?.map(getRId).join(',') || '';
        const sId = getSId(uc.subject);

        xml += `  <TanimliDers id="${lessonIdCounter++}" Ogretmenler="${tIds}" Siniflar="${cIds}" Ders="${sId}">\n`;
        xml += `    <Kart Yerlesim="" Saat="${uc.hours}" Derslikler="${rIds}"/>\n  </TanimliDers>\n`;
    });

    xml += `</TanimliDersler>\n</DersProgrami>`;

    const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF' + xml], { type: 'application/xml;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `${schoolInfo.name}_Program_Pro.xml`;
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
    showToast("Program XML formatÄ±nda baÅarÄ±yla dÄ±Åa aktarÄ±ldÄ±.");
  };

  const parseXMLData = (xmlText) => {
    try {
      const parser = new DOMParser();
      const xmlDoc = parser.parseFromString(xmlText, "text/xml");
      if (xmlDoc.getElementsByTagName("parsererror").length > 0) throw new Error("GeÃ§ersiz XML formatÄ±.");

      const ekBilgiler = Array.from(xmlDoc.getElementsByTagName("EkBilgi"));
      let sName = schoolInfo.name;
      let sYear = schoolInfo.year;
      ekBilgiler.forEach(node => {
         if(node.getAttribute("EkBilgiTur") === "1") sName = node.getAttribute("Deger");
         if(node.getAttribute("EkBilgiTur") === "5") sYear = node.getAttribute("Deger");
      });
      setSchoolInfo(prev => ({...prev, name: sName || 'Okul AdÄ±', year: sYear || '2024-2025'}));

      const saatMap = {};
      const newTimes = [...schoolSettings.lessonTimes];
      
      Array.from(xmlDoc.getElementsByTagName("Gun")).forEach((gunNode, dIdx) => {
        Array.from(gunNode.getElementsByTagName("Saat")).forEach((saatNode, pIdx) => {
          saatMap[saatNode.getAttribute("id")] = { dIdx, pIdx };
          if (dIdx === 0 && saatNode.getAttribute("Baslangic")) {
             newTimes[pIdx] = { start: saatNode.getAttribute("Baslangic"), end: saatNode.getAttribute("Bitis") || '' };
          }
        });
      });
      setSchoolSettings({...schoolSettings, lessonTimes: newTimes});

      const tMap: Record<string, string> = {}; Array.from(xmlDoc.getElementsByTagName("Ogretmen")).forEach(n => { const id = n.getAttribute("id"); if (id) tMap[id] = (n.getAttribute("KisaAdi") || n.getAttribute("Adi") || '').toUpperCase(); });
      const cMap: Record<string, string> = {}; Array.from(xmlDoc.getElementsByTagName("Sinif")).forEach(n => { const id = n.getAttribute("id"); if (id) cMap[id] = (n.getAttribute("KisaAdi") || n.getAttribute("Adi") || '').toUpperCase(); });
      const sMap: Record<string, string> = {}; Array.from(xmlDoc.getElementsByTagName("Ders")).forEach(n => { const id = n.getAttribute("id"); if (id) sMap[id] = (n.getAttribute("KisaAdi") || n.getAttribute("Adi") || '').toUpperCase(); });
      const rMap: Record<string, string> = {}; Array.from(xmlDoc.getElementsByTagName("Derslik")).forEach(n => { const id = n.getAttribute("id"); if (id) rMap[id] = (n.getAttribute("KisaAdi") || n.getAttribute("Adi") || '').toUpperCase(); });

      const newSchedules: Record<string, any[][]> = {}; const newClassSchedules: Record<string, any[][]> = {}; const newRoomSchedules: Record<string, any[][]> = {}; const newUnplaced: any[] = [];
      Object.values(tMap).forEach(t => newSchedules[t] = Array.from({length: 7}).map(() => Array(15).fill('')));
      Object.values(cMap).forEach(c => newClassSchedules[c] = Array.from({length: 7}).map(() => Array(15).fill('')));
      Object.values(rMap).forEach(r => newRoomSchedules[r] = Array.from({length: 7}).map(() => Array(15).fill('')));

      let loadedCards = 0;
      Array.from(xmlDoc.getElementsByTagName("TanimliDers")).forEach(dersNode => {
        const teacherIds = dersNode.getAttribute("Ogretmenler")?.split(',') || [];
        const classIds = dersNode.getAttribute("Siniflar")?.split(',') || [];
        const sName = sMap[dersNode.getAttribute("Ders")] || '';
        
        const teacherNames = teacherIds.map(id => tMap[id]).filter(Boolean);
        const classNames = classIds.map(id => cMap[id]).filter(Boolean);

        if (teacherNames.length === 0 || classNames.length === 0 || !sName) return;

        const kartlar = Array.from(dersNode.getElementsByTagName("Kart"));
        if (kartlar.length === 0) newUnplaced.push({ id: generateId(), teachers: teacherNames, classes: classNames, rooms: [], subject: sName, hours: 2 });
        
        kartlar.forEach(kart => {
          const saatIds = kart.getAttribute("Yerlesim")?.split(',') || [];
          const roomIds = kart.getAttribute("Derslikler")?.split(',') || [];
          const roomNames = roomIds.map(id => rMap[id]).filter(Boolean);

          let hours = saatIds.length;
          let placed = false;
          
          const cardId = generateId();
          const cardData = JSON.stringify({ id: cardId, teachers: teacherNames, classes: classNames, rooms: roomNames, subject: sName });

          saatIds.forEach(saatId => {
            const pos = saatMap[saatId];
            if (pos && pos.dIdx < 7 && pos.pIdx < 15) {
              teacherNames.forEach(t => { if(newSchedules[t]) newSchedules[t][pos.dIdx][pos.pIdx] = cardData; });
              classNames.forEach(c => { if(newClassSchedules[c]) newClassSchedules[c][pos.dIdx][pos.pIdx] = cardData; });
              roomNames.forEach(r => { if(newRoomSchedules[r]) newRoomSchedules[r][pos.dIdx][pos.pIdx] = cardData; });
              placed = true;
              loadedCards++;
            }
          });
          
          if(!placed && hours > 0) newUnplaced.push({ id: generateId(), teachers: teacherNames, classes: classNames, rooms: roomNames, subject: sName, hours: hours });
        });
      });

      setTeachers(Object.values(tMap).sort((a,b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'tr')));
      setClasses(Object.values(cMap).sort((a,b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'tr')));
      setSubjects(Object.values(sMap).sort((a,b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'tr')));
      setRooms(Object.values(rMap).sort((a,b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'tr')));
      
      setSchedules(newSchedules);
      setClassSchedules(newClassSchedules);
      setRoomSchedules(newRoomSchedules);
      setUnplacedCourses(newUnplaced);
      setConstraints({ teachers: {}, classes: {}, subjects: {}, rooms: {} }); 
      
      showToast(`XML BaÅarÄ±yla YÃ¼klendi! ${loadedCards} adet matris hÃ¼cresi yerleÅti.`);
      setMainTab('matrix');
    } catch (error) { showToast("Dosya Okuma HatasÄ±: " + error.message, "error"); }
  };
  const generateReportHTML = (type, forExcel = false) => {
    const isTeacher = type.includes('ogretmen');
    const isClass = type.includes('sinif');
    const isRoom = type.includes('derslik');
    const isGenel = type.includes('genel');
    const activeDays = schoolSettings.weekDays.filter(d => d.active);
    const maxPeriods = Math.max(...activeDays.map(d => d.periods));
    
    let dataMaster = isTeacher ? schedules : isClass ? classSchedules : roomSchedules;
    let rowKeys = isTeacher ? teachers : isClass ? classes : rooms;
    let reportTitle = isTeacher ? 'ÃÄRETMEN' : isClass ? 'SINIF' : 'DERSLÄ°K';

    let htmlContent = '';

    if (!forExcel) {
      htmlContent += `
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html><head><title>Ders ProgramÄ± - ${schoolInfo.name}</title>
        <style>
          @page { size: A4 landscape; margin: 8mm; }
          html, body { width: 100%; margin: 0; padding: 0; font-family: 'Segoe UI', Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif; background: #fff; color: #000; }
          .page-container { width: 100%; page-break-after: always; display: block; box-sizing: border-box; padding: 0; margin-bottom: 20px; }
          .page-container:last-child { page-break-after: auto; }
          .header { text-align: center; margin-bottom: 10px; border-bottom: 2px solid #000; padding-bottom: 5px; flex-shrink: 0; }
          .header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 900; letter-spacing: 1px; }
          .header h3 { margin: 3px 0; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; color: #333; }
          .header h2 { margin: 8px 0 0 0; font-size: 16px; font-weight: 800; background: #f1f5f9; display: inline-block; padding: 4px 16px; border-radius: 4px; border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; }
          table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; table-layout: fixed; margin-bottom: 10px; page-break-inside: auto; }
          tr { page-break-inside: avoid; page-break-after: auto; }
          th, td { border: 1px solid #64748b; text-align: center; overflow: hidden; padding: 4px; vertical-align: middle; word-wrap: break-word; }
          th { background-color: #e2e8f0 !important; font-weight: 800; font-size: 12.5px; color: #0f172a; -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; print-color-adjust: exact; }
          .row-header { font-weight: 800; text-align: left; background-color: #f8fafc !important; width: 120px; font-size: 12px; color: #0f172a; padding-left: 6px; -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; print-color-adjust: exact; border-right: 2px solid #64748b; }
          .time-header { font-size: 10px; color: #475569; display: block; font-weight: 600; margin-top: 2px;}
          .signature-area { display: table; width: 100%; margin-top: 30px; page-break-inside: avoid; padding-top: 20px; }
          .signature-left { display: table-cell; text-align: center; width: 50%; font-size: 13px; }
          .signature-right { display: table-cell; text-align: center; width: 50%; font-size: 13px; }
          
          .cell-content { display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: center; align-items: center; height: 100%; gap: 3px; }
          .main-text { font-weight: 900; font-size: clamp(10px, 1.2vw, 14px); color: #0f172a; line-height: 1.1; }
          .sub-text { font-size: clamp(9px, 1vw, 12px); color: #334155; font-weight: 700; line-height: 1.1; }
          .room-text { font-size: clamp(8px, 0.9vw, 10px); color: #64748b; font-style: italic; line-height: 1.1; }
          
          .genel-table th { font-size: clamp(8px, 0.9vw, 11px); padding: 3px; background-color: #f1f5f9 !important; border: 1px solid #94a3b8; }
          .genel-table td { padding: 3px; border: 1px solid #94a3b8; }
          .genel-table .row-header { font-size: clamp(8px, 0.9vw, 11px); width: clamp(60px, 8vw, 95px); font-weight: 900; background-color: #f8fafc !important; border-right: 2px solid #94a3b8; }
          .genel-table .main-text { font-size: clamp(7px, 0.8vw, 10px); font-weight: 800; line-height: 1.1; display: block; padding-bottom: 2px; }
          .genel-table .sub-text { font-size: clamp(6px, 0.7vw, 8px); color: #475569; font-weight: 600; line-height: 1; display: block; }
          
          .text-cell { mso-number-format:"\@"; }
        </style>
        </head><body>
      `;
    } else {
      htmlContent += `
        <html xmlns:o="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:office" xmlns:x="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:excel" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/TR/REC-html40">
        <head><meta charset="utf-8" />
          <style>
            table { border-collapse: collapse; font-family: Calibri, sans-serif; font-size: 12px; width: 100%; }
            th, td { border: 1px solid #000000; padding: 6px; text-align: center; vertical-align: middle; }
            th { background-color: #d1d5db; font-weight: bold; }
            .row-header { font-weight: bold; text-align: left; background-color: #f1f5f9; }
            .header { text-align: center; font-weight: bold; font-size: 16px; margin-bottom: 20px;}
            .empty-row td { border: none; height: 30px; }
            .text-cell { mso-number-format:"\@"; }
          </style>
        </head><body>
      `;
    }

    const getHeaderHtml = (title) => `
        <div class="header">
            <h1>${schoolInfo.name}</h1>
            <h3>${schoolInfo.year} EÄitim ÃÄretim YÄ±lÄ±</h3>
            <h2>${title}</h2>
        </div>
    `;

    const getSignatureHtml = (rowKey = null) => {
        let teacherHtml = '';
        if (isTeacher && !isGenel && rowKey) {
            teacherHtml = `
               <td colspan="2" style="border: none; text-align: left; vertical-align: bottom;">
                 <strong>AslÄ±nÄ± elden teslim aldÄ±m.</strong><br/>
                 Tarih: ...../...../20....<br/><br/><br/>
                 ${rowKey}
               </td>
            `;
        } else {
            teacherHtml = `<td colspan="2" style="border: none;"></td>`;
        }

        if (forExcel) {
            // How many columns total? 1 for SAAT + activeDays.length
            const totalCols = activeDays.length + 1;
            // Distribute across totalCols. Let's say we have 6 columns.
            // teacherHtml can take 2, vice takes 2, principal takes 2.
            // We just use colspan.
            const col1 = Math.floor(totalCols / 3) || 1;
            const col2 = Math.floor(totalCols / 3) || 1;
            const col3 = totalCols - col1 - col2;
            
            let tHtml = '';
            if (isTeacher && !isGenel && rowKey) {
                tHtml = `
                   <td colspan="${col1}" style="border: none; text-align: left; vertical-align: bottom; font-size: 14px;">
                     <br/><br/>
                     <strong>AslÄ±nÄ± elden teslim aldÄ±m.</strong><br/>
                     Tarih: ...../...../20....<br/><br/><br/><br/>
                     <strong>${rowKey}</strong>
                   </td>
                `;
            } else {
                tHtml = `<td colspan="${col1}" style="border: none;"></td>`;
            }

            return `
              <tr style="border: none;">
                 ${tHtml}
                 <td colspan="${col2}" style="border: none; text-align: center; vertical-align: bottom; font-size: 14px;">
                    <br/><br/>
                    <strong>MÃ¼dÃ¼r YardÄ±mcÄ±sÄ±</strong><br/><br/><br/><br/><strong>${schoolInfo.vicePrincipal || '...........................'}</strong>
                 </td>
                 <td colspan="${col3}" style="border: none; text-align: center; vertical-align: bottom; font-size: 14px;">
                    <br/><br/>
                    <strong>Okul MÃ¼dÃ¼rÃ¼</strong><br/><br/><br/><br/><strong>${schoolInfo.principal || '...........................'}</strong>
                 </td>
              </tr>
            `;
        } else {
            return `
            <table style="width: 100%; border: none; margin-top: auto; page-break-inside: avoid; font-size: 13px;">
               <tr style="border: none;">
                  <td style="border: none; width: 33%; text-align: left; vertical-align: bottom;">
                     ${isTeacher && !isGenel && rowKey ? `<strong>AslÄ±nÄ± elden teslim aldÄ±m.</strong><br/><br/>Tarih: ...../...../20....<br/><br/><br/><strong>${rowKey}</strong>` : ''}
                  </td>
                  <td style="border: none; width: 33%; text-align: center; vertical-align: bottom;">
                     <strong>MÃ¼dÃ¼r YardÄ±mcÄ±sÄ±</strong><br/><br/><br/><br/><strong>${schoolInfo.vicePrincipal || '...........................'}</strong>
                  </td>
                  <td style="border: none; width: 33%; text-align: right; vertical-align: bottom;">
                     <strong>Okul MÃ¼dÃ¼rÃ¼</strong><br/><br/><br/><br/><strong>${schoolInfo.principal || '...........................'}</strong>
                  </td>
               </tr>
            </table>
            `;
        }
    };

    if (isGenel) {
       htmlContent += `<div class="page-container">`;
       htmlContent += getHeaderHtml(`Genel ${reportTitle} ProgramÄ±`);
       htmlContent += `<table class="genel-table"><thead><tr><th rowspan="2" class="row-header">${reportTitle}</th>`;
       activeDays.forEach(day => htmlContent += `<th colspan="${day.periods}">${day.name}</th>`);
       htmlContent += `</tr><tr>`;
       activeDays.forEach(day => {
         for(let i=0; i<day.periods; i++) {
           htmlContent += `<th>${i+1}<br/><span class="time-header">${schoolSettings.lessonTimes[i].start}</span></th>`;
         }
       });
       htmlContent += `</tr></thead><tbody>`;

       rowKeys.forEach(rowKey => {
         htmlContent += `<tr><td class="row-header text-cell">${rowKey}</td>`;
         activeDays.forEach((day, dIdx) => {
           for(let i=0; i<day.periods; i++) {
             const valStr = dataMaster[rowKey]?.[dIdx]?.[i];
             let displayVal = '';
             if(valStr && valStr !== '') {
                 const cData = parseCellData(valStr);
                 if (cData) {
